import { getServices } from "#src/server/context.ts";

const handleAuth = async (request: Request) =>
  await getServices().auth.handler(request);

export const GET = handleAuth;
export const POST = handleAuth;

#   next-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   next-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import type { Route } from "next";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import { AuthForm } from "#src/components/auth-form.tsx";
import { getSession } from "#src/server/session.ts";

const redirectTargets: Route[] = ["/"];

const safeRedirect = (value: string | string[] | undefined) =>
  redirectTargets.find((target) => target === value) ?? "/";

const LoginPage = async ({ searchParams }: PageProps<"/login">) => {
  const { redirect: target } = await searchParams;
  const redirectTo = safeRedirect(target);

  if (await getSession()) {
    redirect(redirectTo);
  }

  return <AuthForm redirectTo={redirectTo} />;
};

export default LoginPage;

import type { Route } from "next";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import { AuthForm } from "#src/components/auth-form.tsx";
import { getSession } from "#src/server/session.ts";

const redirectTargets: Route[] = ["/", "/todos"];

const safeRedirect = (value: string | string[] | undefined) =>
  redirectTargets.find((target) => target === value) ?? "/todos";

const LoginPage = async ({ searchParams }: PageProps<"/login">) => {
  const { redirect: target } = await searchParams;
  const redirectTo = safeRedirect(target);

  if (await getSession()) {
    redirect(redirectTo);
  }

  return <AuthForm redirectTo={redirectTo} />;
};

export default LoginPage;

import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";

import { DotGrid } from "#src/components/dot-grid.tsx";
import { StackPlate } from "#src/components/stack-plate.tsx";

// Bottom to top. The top plate drifts first and each one below follows it.
const plates = [0.75, 0.5, 0.25, 0];

/** The `dark` class holds this panel to the dark tokens under either theme. */
export const AuthArt = () => {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();

  return (
    <aside className="dark bg-card text-card-foreground relative isolate hidden flex-col justify-between gap-8 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border p-10 lg:flex">
      <DotGrid />
      <div
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="flex flex-1 flex-col-reverse items-center justify-center"
      >
        {plates.map((delay, index) => (
          <motion.div
            animate={reduced === true ? {} : { y: [0, -12, 0] }}
            className="w-64 not-first:-mb-24"
            key={delay}
            transition={{
              delay,
              duration: 5,
              ease: "easeInOut",
              repeat: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
            }}
          >
            <StackPlate active={index === plates.length - 1} />
          </motion.div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <p className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-balance">
          Your accounts, in your own database.
        </p>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
          Sessions are stored beside the rest of your data.
        </p>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

#   next-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker

"use client";

import { CircleAlertIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { motion } from "motion/react";
import type { Route } from "next";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import type { SubmitEvent } from "react";

import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@my-app/ui/components/alert";
import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import {
  Field,
  FieldDescription,
  FieldGroup,
  FieldLabel,
} from "@my-app/ui/components/field";
import { Input } from "@my-app/ui/components/input";
import { Spinner } from "@my-app/ui/components/spinner";

import { AuthArt } from "#src/components/auth-art.tsx";
import { StackPlate } from "#src/components/stack-plate.tsx";
import { authClient } from "#src/lib/auth-client.ts";

type Mode = "sign-in" | "sign-up";

const copy = {
  "sign-in": {
    description: "Sign in with your email and password.",
    submit: "Sign in",
    switchLabel: "Need an account? Sign up",
    title: "Welcome back",
  },
  "sign-up": {
    description: "Sign up with your email and a password.",
    submit: "Sign up",
    switchLabel: "Already have an account? Sign in",
    title: "Create an account",
  },
} as const satisfies Record<Mode, Record<string, string>>;

const formValue = (form: FormData, name: string) => {
  const value = form.get(name);

  return value === null || value instanceof File ? "" : value;
};

const request = async (mode: Mode, form: FormData) => {
  const email = formValue(form, "email");
  const password = formValue(form, "password");

  return mode === "sign-in"
    ? await authClient.signIn.email({ email, password })
    : await authClient.signUp.email({
        email,
        name: formValue(form, "name"),
        password,
      });
};

/** Signs in or up, and returns the message to show when that fails. */
const authenticate = async (mode: Mode, form: FormData) => {
  try {
    const { error } = await request(mode, form);

    return error ? (error.message ?? error.statusText) : null;
  } catch {
    return "Could not reach the server. Check your connection and try again.";
  }
};

export const AuthForm = ({ redirectTo }: { redirectTo: Route }) => {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("sign-in");
  const text = copy[mode];

  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  const handleSubmit = (event: SubmitEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);

    setError(null);
    startTransition(async () => {
      const failure = await authenticate(mode, form);

      if (failure === null) {
        router.push(redirectTo);
        router.refresh();

        return;
      }

      setError(failure);
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-10 lg:min-h-160 lg:grid-cols-2">
      <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col justify-center gap-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <div className="mb-4 w-16 lg:hidden">
            <StackPlate active />
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
            {text.title}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">{text.description}</p>
        </div>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <fieldset disabled={isPending}>
            <FieldGroup>
              {error !== null && (
                <Alert variant="destructive">
                  <CircleAlertIcon />
                  <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              )}
              {mode === "sign-up" && (
                <motion.div
                  animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
                  className="-m-1 overflow-hidden p-1"
                  initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                >
                  <Field>
                    <FieldLabel htmlFor="name">Name</FieldLabel>
                    <Input autoComplete="name" id="name" name="name" required />
                  </Field>
                </motion.div>
              )}
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="email">Email</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  autoComplete="email"
                  id="email"
                  name="email"
                  placeholder="you@example.com"
                  required
                  type="email"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="password">Password</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  autoComplete={
                    mode === "sign-in" ? "current-password" : "new-password"
                  }
                  id="password"
                  minLength={8}
                  name="password"
                  required
                  type="password"
                />
                {mode === "sign-up" && (
                  <FieldDescription>At least 8 characters.</FieldDescription>
                )}
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <Button size="lg" type="submit">
                  {isPending && <Spinner data-icon="inline-start" />}
                  {text.submit}
                </Button>
                <Button
                  onClick={() => {
                    setError(null);
                    setMode(mode === "sign-in" ? "sign-up" : "sign-in");
                  }}
                  type="button"
                  variant="link"
                >
                  {text.switchLabel}
                </Button>
              </Field>
            </FieldGroup>
          </fieldset>
        </form>
      </div>
      <AuthArt />
    </div>
  );
};

# apps/web/src/components/auth-form.tsx · 16 stacks
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e

#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { CircleAlertIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { motion } from "motion/react";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import type { SubmitEvent } from "react";

import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@my-app/ui/components/alert";
import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import {
  Field,
  FieldDescription,
  FieldGroup,
  FieldLabel,
} from "@my-app/ui/components/field";
import { Input } from "@my-app/ui/components/input";
import { Spinner } from "@my-app/ui/components/spinner";

import { AuthArt } from "#src/components/auth-art.tsx";
import { StackPlate } from "#src/components/stack-plate.tsx";
import { authClient } from "#src/lib/auth.ts";

type Mode = "sign-in" | "sign-up";

const copy = {
  "sign-in": {
    description: "Sign in with your email and password.",
    submit: "Sign in",
    switchLabel: "Need an account? Sign up",
    title: "Welcome back",
  },
  "sign-up": {
    description: "Sign up with your email and a password.",
    submit: "Sign up",
    switchLabel: "Already have an account? Sign in",
    title: "Create an account",
  },
} as const satisfies Record<Mode, Record<string, string>>;

const formValue = (form: FormData, name: string) => {
  const value = form.get(name);

  return value === null || value instanceof File ? "" : value;
};

const request = async (mode: Mode, form: FormData) => {
  const email = formValue(form, "email");
  const password = formValue(form, "password");

  return mode === "sign-in"
    ? await authClient.signIn.email({ email, password })
    : await authClient.signUp.email({
        email,
        name: formValue(form, "name"),
        password,
      });
};

/** Signs in or up, and returns the message to show when that fails. */
const authenticate = async (mode: Mode, form: FormData) => {
  try {
    const { error } = await request(mode, form);

    return error ? (error.message ?? error.statusText) : null;
  } catch {
    return "Could not reach the server. Check your connection and try again.";
  }
};

export const AuthForm = ({ onSuccess }: { onSuccess: () => Promise<void> }) => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("sign-in");
  const text = copy[mode];

  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  const handleSubmit = (event: SubmitEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);

    setError(null);
    startTransition(async () => {
      const failure = await authenticate(mode, form);

      if (failure === null) {
        await onSuccess();

        return;
      }

      setError(failure);
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-10 lg:min-h-160 lg:grid-cols-2">
      <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col justify-center gap-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <div className="mb-4 w-16 lg:hidden">
            <StackPlate active />
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
            {text.title}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">{text.description}</p>
        </div>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <fieldset disabled={isPending}>
            <FieldGroup>
              {error !== null && (
                <Alert variant="destructive">
                  <CircleAlertIcon />
                  <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              )}
              {mode === "sign-up" && (
                <motion.div
                  animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
                  className="-m-1 overflow-hidden p-1"
                  initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                >
                  <Field>
                    <FieldLabel htmlFor="name">Name</FieldLabel>
                    <Input autoComplete="name" id="name" name="name" required />
                  </Field>
                </motion.div>
              )}
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="email">Email</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  autoComplete="email"
                  id="email"
                  name="email"
                  placeholder="you@example.com"
                  required
                  type="email"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="password">Password</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  autoComplete={
                    mode === "sign-in" ? "current-password" : "new-password"
                  }
                  id="password"
                  minLength={8}
                  name="password"
                  required
                  type="password"
                />
                {mode === "sign-up" && (
                  <FieldDescription>At least 8 characters.</FieldDescription>
                )}
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <Button size="lg" type="submit">
                  {isPending && <Spinner data-icon="inline-start" />}
                  {text.submit}
                </Button>
                <Button
                  onClick={() => {
                    setError(null);
                    setMode(mode === "sign-in" ? "sign-up" : "sign-in");
                  }}
                  type="button"
                  variant="link"
                >
                  {text.switchLabel}
                </Button>
              </Field>
            </FieldGroup>
          </fieldset>
        </form>
      </div>
      <AuthArt />
    </div>
  );
};

import { useHydrated } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { CircleAlertIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { motion } from "motion/react";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import type { SubmitEvent } from "react";

import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@my-app/ui/components/alert";
import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import {
  Field,
  FieldDescription,
  FieldGroup,
  FieldLabel,
} from "@my-app/ui/components/field";
import { Input } from "@my-app/ui/components/input";
import { Spinner } from "@my-app/ui/components/spinner";

import { AuthArt } from "#src/components/auth-art.tsx";
import { StackPlate } from "#src/components/stack-plate.tsx";
import { authClient } from "#src/lib/auth.ts";

type Mode = "sign-in" | "sign-up";

const copy = {
  "sign-in": {
    description: "Sign in with your email and password.",
    submit: "Sign in",
    switchLabel: "Need an account? Sign up",
    title: "Welcome back",
  },
  "sign-up": {
    description: "Sign up with your email and a password.",
    submit: "Sign up",
    switchLabel: "Already have an account? Sign in",
    title: "Create an account",
  },
} as const satisfies Record<Mode, Record<string, string>>;

const formValue = (form: FormData, name: string) => {
  const value = form.get(name);

  return value === null || value instanceof File ? "" : value;
};

const request = async (mode: Mode, form: FormData) => {
  const email = formValue(form, "email");
  const password = formValue(form, "password");

  return mode === "sign-in"
    ? await authClient.signIn.email({ email, password })
    : await authClient.signUp.email({
        email,
        name: formValue(form, "name"),
        password,
      });
};

/** Signs in or up, and returns the message to show when that fails. */
const authenticate = async (mode: Mode, form: FormData) => {
  try {
    const { error } = await request(mode, form);

    return error ? (error.message ?? error.statusText) : null;
  } catch {
    return "Could not reach the server. Check your connection and try again.";
  }
};

export const AuthForm = ({ onSuccess }: { onSuccess: () => Promise<void> }) => {
  const hydrated = useHydrated();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("sign-in");
  const text = copy[mode];

  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  const handleSubmit = (event: SubmitEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);

    setError(null);
    startTransition(async () => {
      const failure = await authenticate(mode, form);

      if (failure === null) {
        await onSuccess();

        return;
      }

      setError(failure);
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-10 lg:min-h-160 lg:grid-cols-2">
      <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col justify-center gap-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <div className="mb-4 w-16 lg:hidden">
            <StackPlate active />
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
            {text.title}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">{text.description}</p>
        </div>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <fieldset disabled={!hydrated || isPending}>
            <FieldGroup>
              {error !== null && (
                <Alert variant="destructive">
                  <CircleAlertIcon />
                  <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              )}
              {mode === "sign-up" && (
                <motion.div
                  animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
                  className="-m-1 overflow-hidden p-1"
                  initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                >
                  <Field>
                    <FieldLabel htmlFor="name">Name</FieldLabel>
                    <Input autoComplete="name" id="name" name="name" required />
                  </Field>
                </motion.div>
              )}
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="email">Email</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  autoComplete="email"
                  id="email"
                  name="email"
                  placeholder="you@example.com"
                  required
                  type="email"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <FieldLabel htmlFor="password">Password</FieldLabel>
                <Input
                  autoComplete={
                    mode === "sign-in" ? "current-password" : "new-password"
                  }
                  id="password"
                  minLength={8}
                  name="password"
                  required
                  type="password"
                />
                {mode === "sign-up" && (
                  <FieldDescription>At least 8 characters.</FieldDescription>
                )}
              </Field>
              <Field>
                <Button size="lg" type="submit">
                  {isPending && <Spinner data-icon="inline-start" />}
                  {text.submit}
                </Button>
                <Button
                  onClick={() => {
                    setError(null);
                    setMode(mode === "sign-in" ? "sign-up" : "sign-in");
                  }}
                  type="button"
                  variant="link"
                >
                  {text.switchLabel}
                </Button>
              </Field>
            </FieldGroup>
          </fieldset>
        </form>
      </div>
      <AuthArt />
    </div>
  );
};

#   next-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   next-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
"use client";

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { LogOutIcon } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { Avatar, AvatarFallback } from "@my-app/ui/components/avatar";
import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuGroup,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@my-app/ui/components/dropdown-menu";

import { authClient } from "#src/lib/auth-client.ts";

interface UserMenuProps {
  user: { name: string; email: string } | null;
}

const graphemes = new Intl.Segmenter(undefined, { granularity: "grapheme" });

// The first letter of each of the first two words: "Ada Lovelace" is "AL".
const initials = (name: string) =>
  name
    .trim()
    .split(/\s+/u)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map(
      (word) =>
        graphemes.segment(word).containing(0)?.segment.toUpperCase() ?? ""
    )
    .join("");

export const UserMenu = ({ user }: UserMenuProps) => {
  const router = useRouter();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const pathname = usePathname();

  const signOut = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      const { error } = await authClient.signOut();

      if (error) {
        throw new Error("Could not sign out. Please try again.");
      }
    },
    onError: () => {
      toast.error("Could not sign out. Please try again.");
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.clear();
      router.push("/");
      router.refresh();
    },
  });

  if (!user) {
    return pathname === "/login" ? null : (
      <Button
        nativeButton={false}
        render={<Link href="/login" />}
        size="sm"
        variant="outline"
      >
        Sign in
      </Button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger render={<Button size="sm" variant="ghost" />}>
        <Avatar aria-hidden="true" data-icon="inline-start" size="sm">
          <AvatarFallback>{initials(user.name)}</AvatarFallback>
        </Avatar>
        <span className="sr-only sm:not-sr-only sm:max-w-40 sm:truncate">
          {user.name}
        </span>
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>
      <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="min-w-56">
        <DropdownMenuGroup>
          <DropdownMenuLabel className="flex flex-col">
            <span className="text-foreground font-medium">{user.name}</span>
            <span className="truncate">{user.email}</span>
          </DropdownMenuLabel>
        </DropdownMenuGroup>
        <DropdownMenuSeparator />
        <DropdownMenuItem
          disabled={signOut.isPending}
          onClick={() => {
            signOut.mutate();
          }}
          variant="destructive"
        >
          <LogOutIcon />
          Sign out
        </DropdownMenuItem>
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  );
};

#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import {
  Link,
  useLocation,
  useRouteContext,
  useRouter,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { LogOutIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useTransition } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { Avatar, AvatarFallback } from "@my-app/ui/components/avatar";
import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuGroup,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@my-app/ui/components/dropdown-menu";

import { authClient } from "#src/lib/auth.ts";

const graphemes = new Intl.Segmenter(undefined, { granularity: "grapheme" });

// The first letter of each of the first two words: "Ada Lovelace" is "AL".
const initials = (name: string) =>
  name
    .trim()
    .split(/\s+/u)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map(
      (word) =>
        graphemes.segment(word).containing(0)?.segment.toUpperCase() ?? ""
    )
    .join("");

export const UserMenu = () => {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const { session } = useRouteContext({ from: "__root__" });
  const pathname = useLocation({ select: (location) => location.pathname });

  const signOut = async () => {
    try {
      const { error } = await authClient.signOut();

      if (error) {
        toast.error("Could not sign out. Please try again.");

        return;
      }

      await router.invalidate();
      await router.navigate({ to: "/" });
    } catch {
      toast.error("Could not sign out. Please try again.");
    }
  };

  if (!session) {
    return pathname === "/login" ? null : (
      <Button
        nativeButton={false}
        render={<Link to="/login" />}
        size="sm"
        variant="outline"
      >
        Sign in
      </Button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger render={<Button size="sm" variant="ghost" />}>
        <Avatar aria-hidden="true" data-icon="inline-start" size="sm">
          <AvatarFallback>{initials(session.user.name)}</AvatarFallback>
        </Avatar>
        <span className="sr-only sm:not-sr-only sm:max-w-40 sm:truncate">
          {session.user.name}
        </span>
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>
      <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="min-w-56">
        <DropdownMenuGroup>
          <DropdownMenuLabel className="flex flex-col">
            <span className="text-foreground font-medium">
              {session.user.name}
            </span>
            <span className="truncate">{session.user.email}</span>
          </DropdownMenuLabel>
        </DropdownMenuGroup>
        <DropdownMenuSeparator />
        <DropdownMenuItem
          disabled={pending}
          onClick={() => {
            startTransition(signOut);
          }}
          variant="destructive"
        >
          <LogOutIcon />
          Sign out
        </DropdownMenuItem>
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  );
};

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  Link,
  useLocation,
  useRouteContext,
  useRouter,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { LogOutIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { Avatar, AvatarFallback } from "@my-app/ui/components/avatar";
import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuGroup,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@my-app/ui/components/dropdown-menu";

import { authClient } from "#src/lib/auth.ts";

const graphemes = new Intl.Segmenter(undefined, { granularity: "grapheme" });

// The first letter of each of the first two words: "Ada Lovelace" is "AL".
const initials = (name: string) =>
  name
    .trim()
    .split(/\s+/u)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map(
      (word) =>
        graphemes.segment(word).containing(0)?.segment.toUpperCase() ?? ""
    )
    .join("");

export const UserMenu = () => {
  const router = useRouter();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { session } = useRouteContext({ from: "__root__" });
  const pathname = useLocation({ select: (location) => location.pathname });

  const signOut = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      const { error } = await authClient.signOut();

      if (error) {
        throw new Error("Could not sign out. Please try again.");
      }
    },
    onError: () => {
      toast.error("Could not sign out. Please try again.");
    },
    onSuccess: async () => {
      queryClient.clear();
      await router.invalidate();
      await router.navigate({ to: "/" });
    },
  });

  if (!session) {
    return pathname === "/login" ? null : (
      <Button
        nativeButton={false}
        render={<Link to="/login" />}
        size="sm"
        variant="outline"
      >
        Sign in
      </Button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger render={<Button size="sm" variant="ghost" />}>
        <Avatar aria-hidden="true" data-icon="inline-start" size="sm">
          <AvatarFallback>{initials(session.user.name)}</AvatarFallback>
        </Avatar>
        <span className="sr-only sm:not-sr-only sm:max-w-40 sm:truncate">
          {session.user.name}
        </span>
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>
      <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="min-w-56">
        <DropdownMenuGroup>
          <DropdownMenuLabel className="flex flex-col">
            <span className="text-foreground font-medium">
              {session.user.name}
            </span>
            <span className="truncate">{session.user.email}</span>
          </DropdownMenuLabel>
        </DropdownMenuGroup>
        <DropdownMenuSeparator />
        <DropdownMenuItem
          disabled={signOut.isPending}
          onClick={() => {
            signOut.mutate();
          }}
          variant="destructive"
        >
          <LogOutIcon />
          Sign out
        </DropdownMenuItem>
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  );
};

#   spa-hono-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   spa-hono-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { createAuthClient } from "better-auth/react";

export const authClient = createAuthClient();

export type Session = typeof authClient.$Infer.Session;

export const getSession = async (): Promise<Session | null> => {
  const { data, error } = await authClient.getSession();

  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message ?? error.statusText);
  }

  return data;
};

#   tanstack-start-hono-sqlite-better-auth-expo-bun-docker
#   tanstack-start-hono-sqlite-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   tanstack-start-hono-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { getRequestHeaders } from "@tanstack/react-start/server";
import { createAuthClient } from "better-auth/react";

import { env } from "#src/server/env.ts";

export const authClient = createAuthClient();

// Pages render on the web server, which asks the API server for the visitor's session.
export const getSession = createServerFn({ method: "GET" }).handler(
  async () => {
    const { data, error } = await createAuthClient({
      baseURL: env.SERVER_URL,
    }).getSession({
      fetchOptions: {
        headers: { cookie: getRequestHeaders().get("cookie") ?? "" },
      },
    });

    if (error) {
      throw new Error(error.message ?? error.statusText);
    }

    return data;
  }
);

# apps/web/src/routes/_authenticated.tsx · 16 stacks
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-electron-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-electron-docker-e2e
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-electron-expo-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-electron-expo-docker-e2e
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-expo-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-electron-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-electron-docker-e2e
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-electron-expo-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-electron-expo-docker-e2e
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { RegistryProvider } from "@effect/atom-react";
import { createFileRoute, Outlet, redirect } from "@tanstack/react-router";

const AuthenticatedLayout = () => {
  const { session } = Route.useRouteContext();

  return (
    <RegistryProvider key={session.session.id}>
      <Outlet />
    </RegistryProvider>
  );
};

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated")({
  component: AuthenticatedLayout,
  beforeLoad: ({ context, location }) => {
    if (!context.session) {
      throw redirect({ to: "/login", search: { redirect: location.href } });
    }

    return { session: context.session };
  },
});

import { createFileRoute, redirect } from "@tanstack/react-router";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated")({
  beforeLoad: ({ context, location }) => {
    if (!context.session) {
      throw redirect({ to: "/login", search: { redirect: location.href } });
    }

    return { session: context.session };
  },
});

import { api } from "#src/lib/api.ts";

const TodosPage = () => {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState({ title: "" });
  const { title } = draft;
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: todos } = useSuspenseQuery(api.todos.list.queryOptions());

  const invalidateTodos = {
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: api.todos.key() });
    },
  };

  const createTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.create.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const updateTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.update.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const deleteTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.delete.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const done = todos.filter((todo) => todo.completed).length;

  const handleSubmit = (event: SubmitEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    createTodo.mutate(
      { title },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          // Keep whatever was typed while the request was in flight.
          setDraft((current) => (current === draft ? { title: "" } : current));
        },
      }
    );
  };

  return (
    <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">
      <section className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-2xl flex-col gap-8">
        <header className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <h1 className="text-4xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Todos</h1>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
              Private to your account.
            </p>
          </div>
          {todos.length > 0 && (
            <Badge variant="secondary">
              {done} of {todos.length} done
            </Badge>
          )}
        </header>
        {todos.length > 0 && (
          <div className="bg-muted h-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full">
            <motion.div
              animate={{ scaleX: done / todos.length }}
              className="bg-primary h-full origin-left"
              initial={false}
              transition={{ bounce: 0, duration: 0.5, type: "spring" }}
            />
          </div>
        )}
        <Card>
          <CardContent>
            <form className="flex gap-2" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
              <Input
                aria-label="New todo"
                maxLength={200}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setDraft({ title: event.target.value });
                }}
                placeholder="Add a todo"
                value={title}
              />
              <Button
                disabled={createTodo.isPending || title.trim() === ""}
                type="submit"
              >
                {createTodo.isPending ? (
                  <Spinner data-icon="inline-start" />
                ) : (
                  <PlusIcon data-icon="inline-start" />
                )}
                Add
              </Button>
            </form>
          </CardContent>
          <Separator />
          <ul className="-my-4 divide-y empty:hidden">
            <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
              {todos.map((todo) => (
                <motion.li
                  animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
                  className="hover:bg-muted/50 flex items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden px-4 py-3 transition-colors"
                  exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0, paddingBlock: 0 }}
                  initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                  layout="position"
                  transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                  key={todo.id}
                >
                  <Checkbox
                    aria-label={todo.title}
                    checked={todo.completed}
                    onCheckedChange={(completed) => {
                      updateTodo.mutate({ completed, id: todo.id });
                    }}
                  />
                  <span
                    className={cn(
                      "flex-1 text-sm wrap-anywhere",
                      todo.completed &&
                        "text-muted-foreground decoration-muted-foreground/50 line-through"
                    )}
                  >
                    {todo.title}
                  </span>
                  <Button
                    aria-label={`Delete ${todo.title}`}
                    onClick={() => {
                      deleteTodo.mutate({ id: todo.id });
                    }}
                    size="icon-sm"
                    variant="ghost"
                  >
                    <Trash2Icon className="text-muted-foreground" />
                  </Button>
                </motion.li>
              ))}
            </AnimatePresence>
          </ul>
          {todos.length === 0 && (
            <Empty>
              <EmptyHeader>
                <EmptyMedia variant="icon">
                  <ListTodoIcon />
                </EmptyMedia>
                <EmptyTitle>No todos yet.</EmptyTitle>
                <EmptyDescription>
                  Add one above. It is saved through the API to the database.
                </EmptyDescription>
              </EmptyHeader>
            </Empty>
          )}
        </Card>
      </section>
    </MotionConfig>
  );
};

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/todos")({
  loader: async ({ context }) =>
    await context.queryClient.query(api.todos.list.queryOptions()),
  component: TodosPage,
});

#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { createFileRoute, redirect, useRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";

import { AuthForm } from "#src/components/auth-form.tsx";

// Browsers normalize backslashes and strip tabs/newlines before resolving URLs.
const sameOriginPath = /^\/(?!\/)[^\\\t\r\n]*$/u;

const isSameOriginRedirect = (value: unknown): value is string =>
  typeof value === "string" && sameOriginPath.test(value);

interface LoginSearch {
  redirect?: string;
}

const validateSearch = (search: { redirect?: unknown }): LoginSearch => ({
  // TanStack merges validated search with the input, so rejected values must be overwritten.
  redirect: isSameOriginRedirect(search.redirect) ? search.redirect : undefined,
});

const LoginPage = () => {
  const router = useRouter();
  const search = Route.useSearch();

  const handleSuccess = async () => {
    await router.invalidate();
    await router.navigate({ href: search.redirect ?? "/todos" });
  };

  return <AuthForm onSuccess={handleSuccess} />;
};

export const Route = createFileRoute("/login")({
  validateSearch,
  beforeLoad: ({ context, search }) => {
    if (context.session) {
      throw redirect({ href: search.redirect ?? "/todos" });
    }
  },
  component: LoginPage,
});

#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { createFileRoute, redirect, useRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";

import { AuthForm } from "#src/components/auth-form.tsx";

// Browsers normalize backslashes and strip tabs/newlines before resolving URLs.
const sameOriginPath = /^\/(?!\/)[^\\\t\r\n]*$/u;

const isSameOriginRedirect = (value: unknown): value is string =>
  typeof value === "string" && sameOriginPath.test(value);

interface LoginSearch {
  redirect?: string;
}

const validateSearch = (search: { redirect?: unknown }): LoginSearch => ({
  // TanStack merges validated search with the input, so rejected values must be overwritten.
  redirect: isSameOriginRedirect(search.redirect) ? search.redirect : undefined,
});

const LoginPage = () => {
  const router = useRouter();
  const search = Route.useSearch();

  const handleSuccess = async () => {
    await router.invalidate();
    await router.navigate({ href: search.redirect ?? "/" });
  };

  return <AuthForm onSuccess={handleSuccess} />;
};

export const Route = createFileRoute("/login")({
  validateSearch,
  beforeLoad: ({ context, search }) => {
    if (context.session) {
      throw redirect({ href: search.redirect ?? "/" });
    }
  },
  component: LoginPage,
});

import "server-only";
import { createAuthClient } from "better-auth/client";
import { headers } from "next/headers";
import { cache } from "react";

import { readEnv } from "#src/server/env.ts";

// Pages render on the web server, which asks the API server for the visitor's session.
export const getSession = cache(async () => {
  const requestHeaders = await headers();

  const { data, error } = await createAuthClient({
    baseURL: readEnv().SERVER_URL,
  }).getSession({
    fetchOptions: {
      headers: { cookie: requestHeaders.get("cookie") ?? "" },
    },
  });

  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message ?? error.statusText);
  }

  return data;
});

import "server-only";

import { cache } from "react";

import { getServices } from "#src/server/context.ts";

export const getSession = cache(async () => {
  const requestHeaders = await headers();

  return await getServices().auth.api.getSession({ headers: requestHeaders });
});

import { expo } from "@better-auth/expo";
import { mongodbAdapter } from "@better-auth/mongo-adapter";
import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";

import type { Database } from "@my-app/db";

interface AuthOptions {
  db: Database;
  baseURL: string;
  secret: string;
}

export const createAuth = ({ db, baseURL, secret }: AuthOptions) =>
  betterAuth({
    baseURL,
    database: mongodbAdapter(db.connection.getClient().db()),
    emailAndPassword: { enabled: true },
    // The mobile app sends its scheme as the origin; the plugin also trusts exp:// when NODE_ENV is development.
    plugins: [expo()],
    secret,
    trustedOrigins: ["my-app://"],
  });

export type Auth = ReturnType<typeof createAuth>;

export type Session = Auth["$Infer"]["Session"];

import { drizzleAdapter } from "@better-auth/drizzle-adapter/relations-v2";
import { expo } from "@better-auth/expo";
import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";

import type { Database } from "@my-app/db";
import * as schema from "@my-app/db/schema/auth";

interface AuthOptions {
  db: Database;
  baseURL: string;
  secret: string;
}

export const createAuth = ({ db, baseURL, secret }: AuthOptions) =>
  betterAuth({
    baseURL,
    database: drizzleAdapter(db, { provider: "pg", schema }),
    emailAndPassword: { enabled: true },
    // The mobile app sends its scheme as the origin; the plugin also trusts exp:// when NODE_ENV is development.
    plugins: [expo()],
    secret,
    trustedOrigins: ["my-app://"],
  });

export type Auth = ReturnType<typeof createAuth>;

export type Session = Auth["$Infer"]["Session"];

import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";
import { nextCookies } from "better-auth/next-js";

  db: Database;
  baseURL: string;
  secret: string;

  betterAuth({
    baseURL,
    database: mongodbAdapter(db.connection.getClient().db()),

    plugins: [expo(), nextCookies()],

# packages/auth/src/index.ts · 2 stacks
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
import { drizzleAdapter } from "@better-auth/drizzle-adapter/relations-v2";
import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";
import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js";
import { Config, Context, Effect, Layer, Redacted, Schema } from "effect";
import postgres from "postgres";

import { relations } from "@my-app/db/relations";
import * as schema from "@my-app/db/schema/auth";

interface AuthOptions {
  readonly baseURL: string;
  readonly databaseUrl: Redacted.Redacted;
  readonly secret: Redacted.Redacted;
}

// The Drizzle adapter takes a Promise-based database (better-auth#7234), so auth keeps a small pool of its own.
const makeAuth = Effect.fn("Auth.make")(function* (options: AuthOptions) {
  const client = yield* Effect.acquireRelease(
    Effect.sync(() =>
      postgres(Redacted.value(options.databaseUrl), {
        connection: { client_min_messages: "warning" },
        max: 4,
      })
    ),
    (sql) =>
      Effect.promise(async () => {
        await sql.end();
      })
  );

  return betterAuth({
    baseURL: options.baseURL,
    database: drizzleAdapter(drizzle({ client, relations }), {
      provider: "pg",
      schema,
    }),
    emailAndPassword: { enabled: true },
    secret: Redacted.value(options.secret),
  });
});

export class Auth extends Context.Service<
  Auth,
  Effect.Success<ReturnType<typeof makeAuth>>
>()("@my-app/auth/Auth") {
  static readonly layer = (options: AuthOptions) =>
    Layer.effect(Auth, makeAuth(options));

  static readonly layerConfig = Layer.unwrap(
    Effect.map(
      Config.all({
        baseURL: Config.String("BETTER_AUTH_URL"),
        databaseUrl: Config.Redacted("DATABASE_URL"),
        secret: Config.schema(
          Schema.Redacted(Schema.String.check(Schema.isMinLength(32))),
          "BETTER_AUTH_SECRET"
        ),
      }),
      Auth.layer
    )
  );
}

# packages/auth/src/index.ts · 2 stacks
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker-e2e
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";

import { drizzleAdapter } from "@better-auth/drizzle-adapter/relations-v2";
import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";
import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/node-sqlite";
import { Config, Context, Effect, Layer, Redacted, Schema } from "effect";

import { relations } from "@my-app/db/relations";
import * as schema from "@my-app/db/schema/auth";

interface AuthOptions {
  readonly baseURL: string;
  readonly databaseUrl: Redacted.Redacted;
  readonly secret: Redacted.Redacted;
}

// Better Auth expects the official synchronous/Promise adapter, not Effect queries.
const makeAuth = Effect.fn("Auth.make")(function* (options: AuthOptions) {
  const client = yield* Effect.acquireRelease(
    Effect.try(
      () =>
        new DatabaseSync(Redacted.value(options.databaseUrl), { timeout: 5000 })
    ),
    (database) =>
      Effect.sync(() => {
        database.close();
      })
  );

  yield* Effect.try(() => {
    client.exec("pragma journal_mode = wal");
  });

  return betterAuth({
    baseURL: options.baseURL,
    database: drizzleAdapter(drizzle({ client, relations }), {
      provider: "sqlite",
      schema,
    }),
    emailAndPassword: { enabled: true },
    secret: Redacted.value(options.secret),
  });
});

export class Auth extends Context.Service<
  Auth,
  Effect.Success<ReturnType<typeof makeAuth>>
>()("@my-app/auth/Auth") {
  static readonly layer = (options: AuthOptions) =>
    Layer.effect(Auth, makeAuth(options));

  static readonly layerConfig = Layer.unwrap(
    Effect.map(
      Config.all({
        baseURL: Config.String("BETTER_AUTH_URL"),
        databaseUrl: Config.Redacted("DATABASE_URL"),
        secret: Config.schema(
          Schema.Redacted(Schema.String.check(Schema.isMinLength(32))),
          "BETTER_AUTH_SECRET"
        ),
      }),
      Auth.layer
    )
  );
}

import * as schema from "@my-app/db/schema/auth";
import { rendererOrigin } from "@my-app/electron";

interface AuthOptions {
  readonly baseURL: string;
  readonly databaseUrl: Redacted.Redacted;
  readonly secret: Redacted.Redacted;
}

// Better Auth expects the official synchronous/Promise adapter, not Effect queries.
const makeAuth = Effect.fn("Auth.make")(function* (options: AuthOptions) {
  const client = yield* Effect.acquireRelease(
    Effect.try(
      () =>
        new DatabaseSync(Redacted.value(options.databaseUrl), { timeout: 5000 })
    ),
    (database) =>
      Effect.sync(() => {
        database.close();
      })
  );

  yield* Effect.try(() => {
    client.exec("pragma journal_mode = wal");
  });

  return betterAuth({
    baseURL: options.baseURL,
    database: drizzleAdapter(drizzle({ client, relations }), {
      provider: "sqlite",
      schema,
    }),
    emailAndPassword: { enabled: true },
    secret: Redacted.value(options.secret),
    // The mobile app sends its scheme as the origin; the plugin also trusts exp:// when NODE_ENV is development.
    plugins: [expo()],
    trustedOrigins: [rendererOrigin, "my-app://"],
  });
});

export class Auth extends Context.Service<
  Auth,
  Effect.Success<ReturnType<typeof makeAuth>>
>()("@my-app/auth/Auth") {
  static readonly layer = (options: AuthOptions) =>
    Layer.effect(Auth, makeAuth(options));

  static readonly layerConfig = Layer.unwrap(
    Effect.map(
      Config.all({
        baseURL: Config.String("BETTER_AUTH_URL"),
        databaseUrl: Config.Redacted("DATABASE_URL"),
        secret: Config.schema(
          Schema.Redacted(Schema.String.check(Schema.isMinLength(32))),
          "BETTER_AUTH_SECRET"
        ),
      }),
      Auth.layer
    )
  );
}

import { drizzleAdapter } from "@better-auth/drizzle-adapter/relations-v2";
import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";

import type { Database } from "@my-app/db";
import * as schema from "@my-app/db/schema/auth";
import { rendererOrigin } from "@my-app/electron";

interface AuthOptions {
  db: Database;
  baseURL: string;
  secret: string;
}

export const createAuth = ({ db, baseURL, secret }: AuthOptions) =>
  betterAuth({
    baseURL,
    database: drizzleAdapter(db, { provider: "sqlite", schema }),
    emailAndPassword: { enabled: true },
    secret,
    trustedOrigins: [rendererOrigin],
  });

export type Auth = ReturnType<typeof createAuth>;

export type Session = Auth["$Infer"]["Session"];

import { drizzleAdapter } from "@better-auth/drizzle-adapter/relations-v2";
import { expo } from "@better-auth/expo";
import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";

import type { Database } from "@my-app/db";
import * as schema from "@my-app/db/schema/auth";
import { rendererOrigin } from "@my-app/electron";

interface AuthOptions {
  db: Database;
  baseURL: string;
  secret: string;
}

export const createAuth = ({ db, baseURL, secret }: AuthOptions) =>
  betterAuth({
    baseURL,
    database: drizzleAdapter(db, { provider: "sqlite", schema }),
    emailAndPassword: { enabled: true },
    // The mobile app sends its scheme as the origin; the plugin also trusts exp:// when NODE_ENV is development.
    plugins: [expo()],
    secret,
    trustedOrigins: [rendererOrigin, "my-app://"],
  });

export type Auth = ReturnType<typeof createAuth>;

export type Session = Auth["$Infer"]["Session"];
